declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t1417 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncfile.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t1419 = alloca i32
  %t1418 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t1417, ptr %t1419, ptr null)
  %t1420 = icmp ne i32 %t1418, 0
  br i1 %t1420, label %main.async.fail.198, label %main.async.ok.198

  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.198:

  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t1421
}
